# `Assembly.lean` implementation audit by Chapter 1 diagram node

## Authority and update rule

`to_formalize/erdos_64_proof.tex` is the sole authority for mathematical statements, hypotheses, alternatives, order, and terminal behavior. Live Lean declaration types, bodies, exact key lists, call sites, and build results are the authority for what the implementation does. Lean doc comments, block comments, and `-- EG-NODE` annotations are search locators only and are never evidence.

The two tables below are the sole authority for current implementation status and the next unresolved work. Whenever a fact changes, update its paper-label row and every corresponding diagram-node row in the same change. Do not add progress logs, dated snapshots, executive findings, or narrative gap sections.

## Status rubric

Three independent properties are tracked, and passing one never implies another.

**Gate A — mechanical composition.** Whether the Lean term exists, elaborates, and depends on no unfinished producer. Measured by `#print axioms` against a tracer inserted for each undefined frontier producer. Gate A says nothing about what the propositions mean.

**Gate B — manuscript fidelity.** Whether the proposition the row actually publishes — the `Holds` branch of its produced `Spine.Key` — is the manuscript's statement. Because `FactSystem` values are data-free, all mathematical content lives in these propositions. A row whose `Holds` is weaker than the manuscript's lemma, or trivially satisfiable, still composes and still closes, and Gate A cannot detect it. Gate B is established only by reading the proposition against the manuscript.

**Gate C — framework compliance.** Whether the step uses only the canonical `ExactLedger` / `FactManifest` / sealed `FactInputs` / `AtomicCT.run` / `Decision.run` path, states its proposition about the literal active residual from `inputs.current`, and projects every constant from the problem presentation.

“Local” and “Residual-local proof?” require a proposition about the literal active minimal-counterexample residual. “Reads ledger” and “Writes ledger” require semantic-key reads and publication through `FactManifest.Produces` or one arm of `Decision`. “Wired” and “Correctly wired?” require the final endpoint to traverse the exact predecessor and branch. “Legal” and “Custom illegal carrier?” enforce the proof-specific declaration boundary and the canonical APIs.

Status legend: ✅ verified from the current type, body, and build; ⚠ concrete partial implementation or unresolved defect; ❌ absent or verified failure; N/A where the column does not apply; — not assessed in this pass (no verdict is claimed).
